{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /etc/claude-code/.claude/skills/manufacturing-failure-reason-codebook-normalization\n\nThis skill should be considered when you need to normalize, standardize, or correct testing engineers' written failure reasons to match the requirements provided in the product codebooks. Common errors in engineer-written reasons include ambiguous descriptions, missing important words, improper personal writing habits, using wrong abbreviations, improper combining multiple reasons into one sentence without clear spacing or in wrong order, writing wrong station names or model, writing typos, improper combining Chinese and English characters, cross-project differences, and taking wrong products' codebook.\n\nSome codes are defined for specific stations and cannot be used by other stations. If entry.stations is not None, the predicted code should only be considered valid when the record station matches one of the stations listed in entry.stations. Otherwise, the code should be rejected. For each record segment, the system evaluates candidate codes defined in the corresponding product codebook and computes an internal matching score for each candidate. You should consider multiple evidence sources to calculate the score to measure how well a candidate code explains the segment, and normalize the score to a stable range [0.0, 1.0]. Evidence can include text evidence from raw_reason_text (e.g., overlap or fuzzy similarity between span_text and codebook text such as standard_label, keywords_examples, or categories), station compatibility, fail_code alignment, test_item alignment, and conflict cues such as mutually exclusive or contradictory signals. After all candidate codes are scored, sort them in descending order. Let c1 be the top candidate with score s1 and c2 be the second candidate with score s2. When multiple candidates fall within a small margin of the best score, the system applies a deterministic tie-break based on record context (e.g., record_id, segment index, station, fail_code, test_item) to avoid always choosing the same code in near-tie cases while keeping outputs reproducible. To provide convincing answers, add station, fail_code, test_item, a short token overlap cue, or a component reference to the rationale.\n\nUNKNOWN handling: UNKNOWN should be decided based on the best match only (i.e., after ranking), not by marking multiple candidates. If the best-match score is low (weak evidence), output pred_code=\"UNKNOWN\" and pred_label=\"\" to give engineering an alert. When strong positive cues exist (e.g., clear component references), UNKNOWN should be less frequent than in generic or noisy segments.\n\nConfidence calibration: confidence ranges from 0.0 to 1.0 and reflects an engineering confidence level (not a probability). Calibrate confidence from match quality so that UNKNOWN predictions are generally less confident than non-UNKNOWN predictions, and confidence values are not nearly constant. Confidence should show distribution-level separation between UNKNOWN and non-UNKNOWN predictions (e.g., means, quantiles, and diversity), and should be weakly aligned with evidence strength; round confidence to 4 decimals.\n\nHere is a pipeline reference\n1) Load test_center_logs.csv into logs_rows and load each product codebook; build valid_code_set, station_scope_map, and CodebookEntry objects.  \n2) For each record, split raw_reason_text into 1–N segments; each segment uses segment_id=<record_id>-S<i> and keeps an exact substring as span_text.  \n3) For each segment, filter candidates by station scope, then compute match score from combined evidence (text evidence, station compatibility, context alignment, and conflict cues).  \n4) Rank candidates by score; if multiple are within a small margin of the best, choose deterministically using a context-dependent tie-break among near-best station-compatible candidates.  \n5) Output exactly one pred_code/pred_label per segment from the product codebook (or UNKNOWN/\"\" when best evidence is weak) and compute confidence by calibrating match quality with sufficient diversity; round to 4 decimals.\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"894a7f9e-d8f8-4be4-9536-b6c6dc8152ab","uuid":"88812dc5-c874-4ec1-9e59-ed3ac3398acb","isSynthetic":true}

multiple issues\n    result = []\n    for seg in segments:\n        seg = seg.strip()\n        if not seg:\n            continue\n\n        # Check for common duplicate patterns (same text repeated)\n        # e.g., \"治具/探针接触不良，重插后偶尔OK 返修 & 治具/探针接触不良...\"\n        # These should be kept together as one segment if they represent the same issue\n\n        # Check if segment contains markers like \"OK了\", \"已处理\", \"有问题\", etc.\n        # These are status markers, not separate issues\n\n        result.append(seg)\n\n    # If no segments found, return original text\n    if not result:\n        result = [raw_text.strip()]\n\n    return result\n\n# Keyword mappings for different defect types\nDEFECT_KEYWORDS = {\n    'OPEN': ['open', '开路', 'OS', '断开', '断路', 'open circuit'],\n    'SHORT': \"\"\"Compute text similarity score between 0 and 1.\"\"\"\n    t1 = normalize_text(text1)\n    t2 = normalize_text(text2)\n\n    if not t1 or not t2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    # Word overlap\n    words1 = set(re.findall(r'\\w+', t1))\n    words2 = set(re.findall(r'\\w+', t2))\n\n    if not words1 or not words2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = words1 & words2\n    union = words1 | words2\n\n    jaccard = len(intersection) / len(union) if union else 0.0\n\n    # Also check substring containment\n    containment = 0.0\n    if t1 in t2 or t2 in t1:\n        containment = 0.5\n\n    return min(1.0, jaccard + containment)\n\ndef compute_keyword_match(text: str, keywords: List[str]) -> Tuple[float, List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute keyword match score and return matched keywords.\n    Returns (score, list of matched keywords).\n    \"\"\"\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    matched = []\n\n    for kw in keywords:\n        kw_lower = kw.lower()\n        if kw_lower in text_lower:\n            matched.append(kw)\n\n    if not keywords:\n        return 0.0, []\n\n    score = len(matched) / len(keywords)\n    return score, matched\n\ndef detect_defect_type(text: str) -> List[str]:\n    \"\"\"Detect defect types from text based on keywords.\"\"\"\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    detected = []\n\n    for defect_type, keywords in DEFECT_KEYWORDS.items():\n match score for a codebook entry against a segment.\n    Returns (score, rationale_hint).\n    \"\"\"\n    score = 0.0\n    rationale_parts = []\n\n    # 1. Station compatibility check (hard filter but also scoring)\n    station_compatible = not entry.station_scope or station in entry.station_scope\n    if not station_compatible:\n        return 0.0, \"station_incompatible\"\n\n    # 2. Text evidence from keywords\n    kw_score, matched_kws = compute_keyword_match(segment_text, entry.keywords)\n    score += kw_score * 0.35\n    if matched_kws:\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"keywords:[{','.join(matched_kws[:2])}]\")\n\n    # 3. Text evidence from standard_label\n    label_sim = compute_text_similarity(segment_text, entry.standard_label)\n    score += label_sim * 0.25\n\n    # 4. Defect type alignment\n    detected_types = detect_defect_type(segment_text)\n    if entry.category_lv2 in detected_types:\n        score += 0.20\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"category:{entry.category_lv2}\")\n\n    # 5. Component match (if label has component reference)\n    segment_components = extract_components(segment_text)\n    label_components = extract_components(entry.standard_label)\n    if segment_components and label_components:\n        common = set(segment_components) & set(label_components)\n        if common:\n            score += 0.15\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"component:{list(common)[0]}\")\n\n    # 6. Station bonus\n    if station_compatible and entry.station_scope:\n        score += 0.05\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"station:{station}\")\n\n    # Normalize score to [0, 1]\n    score = min(1.0, score)\n\n    rationale = '; '.join(rationale_parts) if rationale_parts else 'weak_match'\n    return score, rationale\n\ndef deterministic_tiebreak(record_id: str, segment_idx: int, codes: List[str]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Deterministic tie-break among near-best candidates.\n    Uses hash of context to select consistently.\n    \"\"\"\n    context = f\"{record_id}_{segment_idx}\"\n    hash_val = int(hashlib.md5(context.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n    return codes[hash_val % len(codes)]\n\ndef find_best_match(\n    segment_text: str,\n    codebook: Dict[str, CodebookEntry],\n    station: str,\n    fail_code: str,\n    test_item: str,\n    record_id: str,\n    segment_idx: int\n) -> Tuple[str, str, float, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Find best matching codebook entry for a segment.\n    Returns (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale).\n    \"\"\"\n    candidates = []\n\n    for code, entry in codebook.items():\n        score, rationale = compute_match_score(segment_text, entry, station, fail_code, test_item)\n        if score > 0:\n            candidates.append((code, entry, score, rationale))\n\n    if not candidates:\n        # No matches at all - UNKNOWN\n        return \"UNKNOWN\", \"\", 0.15, \"no_matching_candidates\"\n\n    # Sort by score descending\n    candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[2], reverse=True)\n\n    best_code, best_entry, best_score, best_rationale = candidates[0]\n\n    # Check for near-ties (within 0.05 margin)\n    near_best = [c for c in candidates if c[2] >= best_score - 0.05]\n\n    if len(near_best) > 1:\n        # Apply deterministic tie-break\n        near_codes = [c[0] for c in near_best]\n        chosen_code = deterministic_tiebreak(record_id, segment_idx, near_codes)\n        for c in near_best:\n            if c[0] == chosen_code:\n                best_code, best_entry, best_score, best_rationale = c\n                break\n\n    # UNKNOWN handling: if best score is too low, mark as UNKNOWN\n    unknown_threshold = 0.20\n\n    if best_score < unknown_threshold:\n        # Weak evidence - output UNKNOWN\n        confidence = round(0.10 + best_score * 0.3, 4)  # Low confidence for UNKNOWN\n        return \"UNKNOWN\", \"\", confidence, f\"weak_evidence;best_score={best_score:.2f}\"\n\n    # Compute confidence from match quality\n    # Ensure separation between UNKNOWN and non-UNKNOWN\n    if best_score >= 0.6:\n        confidence = round(0.75 + (best_score - 0.6) * 0.625, 4)  # 0.75-1.0 range\n    elif best_score >= 0.4:\n        confidence = round(0.55 + (best_score - 0.4) * 1.0, 4)    # 0.55-0.75 range\n    else:\n        confidence = round(0.30 + (best_score - 0.2) * 1.25, 4)   # 0.30-0.55 range\n\n    confidence = min(0.98, max(0.25, confidence))\n\n    # Build detailed rationale\n    rationale = f\"{best_rationale};fail_code:{fail_code};test_item:{test_item}\"\n\n    return best_code, best_entry.standard_label, confidence, rationale\n\ndef process_record(\n    record: dict,\n    codebook: Dict[str, CodebookEntry]\n) -> NormalizedRecord:\n    \"\"\"Process a single record and return normalized result.\"\"\"\n    record_id = record['record_id']\n    product_id = record['product_id']\n    station = record['station']\n    engineer_id = record['engineer_id']\n    raw_reason_text = record['raw_reason_text']\n    fail_code = record.get('fail_code', '')\n    test_item = record.get('test_item', '')\n\n    # Segment the raw text\n    segments = segment_reason_text(raw_reason_text)\n\n    # Deduplicate consecutive identical segments\n    deduped_segments = []\n    for seg in segments:\n        seg = seg.strip()\n        if seg and (not deduped_segments or normalize_text(seg) != normalize_text(deduped_segments[-1])):\n            deduped_segments.append(seg)\n\n    if not deduped_segments:\n        deduped_segments = [raw_reason_text if raw_reason_text else \"\"]\n\n    normalized_segments = []\n    for idx, span_text in enumerate(deduped_segments, start=1):\n        segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{idx}\"\n\n        pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale = find_best_match(\n            span_text,\n            codebook,\n            station,\n            fail_code,\n            test_item,\n            record_id,\n            idx\n        )\n\n        normalized_segments.append(NormalizedSegment(\n            segment_id=segment_id,\n            span_text=span_text,\n            pred_code=pred_code,\n            pred_label=pred_label,\n            confidence=confidence,\n            rationale=rationale\n        ))\n\n    return NormalizedRecord(\n        record_id=record_id,\n        product_id=product_id,\n        station=station,\n        engineer_id=engineer_id,\n        raw_reason_text=raw_reason_text,\n        normalized=normalized_segments\n    )\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading codebooks...\")\n    codebooks_raw = load_all_codebooks()\n\n    # multiple issues\n    result = []\n    for seg in segments:\n        seg = seg.strip()\n        if not seg:\n            continue\n\n        # Check for common duplicate patterns (same text repeated)\n        # e.g., \"治具/探针接触不良，重插后偶尔OK 返修 & 治具/探针接触不良...\"\n        # These should be kept together as one segment if they represent the same issue\n\n        # Check if segment contains markers like \"OK了\", \"已处理\", \"有问题\", etc.\n        # These are status markers, not separate issues\n\n        result.append(seg)\n\n    # If no segments found, return original text\n    if not result:\n        result = [raw_text.strip()]\n\n    return result\n\n# Keyword mappings for different defect types\nDEFECT_KEYWORDS = {\n    'OPEN': ['open', '开路', 'OS', '断开', '断路', 'open circuit'],\n    'SHORT': \"\"\"Compute text similarity score between 0 and 1.\"\"\"\n    t1 = normalize_text(text1)\n    t2 = normalize_text(text2)\n\n    if not t1 or not t2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    # Word overlap\n    words1 = set(re.findall(r'\\w+', t1))\n    words2 = set(re.findall(r'\\w+', t2))\n\n    if not words1 or not words2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = words1 & words2\n    union = words1 | words2\n\n    jaccard = len(intersection) / len(union) if union else 0.0\n\n    # Also check substring containment\n    containment = 0.0\n    if t1 in t2 or t2 in t1:\n        containment = 0.5\n\n    return min(1.0, jaccard + containment)\n\ndef compute_keyword_match(text: str, keywords: List[str]) -> Tuple[float, List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute keyword match score and return matched keywords.\n    Returns (score, list of matched keywords).\n    \"\"\"\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    matched = []\n\n    for kw in keywords:\n        kw_lower = kw.lower()\n        if kw_lower in text_lower:\n            matched.append(kw)\n\n    if not keywords:\n        return 0.0, []\n\n    score = len(matched) / len(keywords)\n    return score, matched\n\ndef detect_defect_type(text: str) -> List[str]:\n    \"\"\"Detect defect types from text based on keywords.\"\"\"\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    detected = []\n\n    for defect_type, keywords in DEFECT_KEYWORDS.items():\n match score for a codebook entry against a segment.\n    Returns (score, rationale_hint).\n    \"\"\"\n    score = 0.0\n    rationale_parts = []\n\n    # 1. Station compatibility check (hard filter but also scoring)\n    station_compatible = not entry.station_scope or station in entry.station_scope\n    if not station_compatible:\n        return 0.0, \"station_incompatible\"\n\n    # 2. Text evidence from keywords\n    kw_score, matched_kws = compute_keyword_match(segment_text, entry.keywords)\n    score += kw_score * 0.35\n    if matched_kws:\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"keywords:[{','.join(matched_kws[:2])}]\")\n\n    # 3. Text evidence from standard_label\n    label_sim = compute_text_similarity(segment_text, entry.standard_label)\n    score += label_sim * 0.25\n\n    # 4. Defect type alignment\n    detected_types = detect_defect_type(segment_text)\n    if entry.category_lv2 in detected_types:\n        score += 0.20\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"category:{entry.category_lv2}\")\n\n    # 5. Component match (if label has component reference)\n    segment_components = extract_components(segment_text)\n    label_components = extract_components(entry.standard_label)\n    if segment_components and label_components:\n        common = set(segment_components) & set(label_components)\n        if common:\n            score += 0.15\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"component:{list(common)[0]}\")\n\n    # 6. Station bonus\n    if station_compatible and entry.station_scope:\n        score += 0.05\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"station:{station}\")\n\n    # Normalize score to [0, 1]\n    score = min(1.0, score)\n\n    rationale = '; '.join(rationale_parts) if rationale_parts else 'weak_match'\n    return score, rationale\n\ndef deterministic_tiebreak(record_id: str, segment_idx: int, codes: List[str]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Deterministic tie-break among near-best candidates.\n    Uses hash of context to select consistently.\n    \"\"\"\n    context = f\"{record_id}_{segment_idx}\"\n    hash_val = int(hashlib.md5(context.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n    return codes[hash_val % len(codes)]\n\ndef find_best_match(\n    segment_text: str,\n    codebook: Dict[str, CodebookEntry],\n    station: str,\n    fail_code: str,\n    test_item: str,\n    record_id: str,\n    segment_idx: int\n) -> Tuple[str, str, float, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Find best matching codebook entry for a segment.\n    Returns (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale).\n    \"\"\"\n    candidates = []\n\n    for code, entry in codebook.items():\n        score, rationale = compute_match_score(segment_text, entry, station, fail_code, test_item)\n        if score > 0:\n            candidates.append((code, entry, score, rationale))\n\n    if not candidates:\n        # No matches at all - UNKNOWN\n        return \"UNKNOWN\", \"\", 0.15, \"no_matching_candidates\"\n\n    # Sort by score descending\n    candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[2], reverse=True)\n\n    best_code, best_entry, best_score, best_rationale = candidates[0]\n\n    # Check for near-ties (within 0.05 margin)\n    near_best = [c for c in candidates if c[2] >= best_score - 0.05]\n\n    if len(near_best) > 1:\n        # Apply deterministic tie-break\n        near_codes = [c[0] for c in near_best]\n        chosen_code = deterministic_tiebreak(record_id, segment_idx, near_codes)\n        for c in near_best:\n            if c[0] == chosen_code:\n                best_code, best_entry, best_score, best_rationale = c\n                break\n\n    # UNKNOWN handling: if best score is too low, mark as UNKNOWN\n    unknown_threshold = 0.20\n\n    if best_score < unknown_threshold:\n        # Weak evidence - output UNKNOWN\n        confidence = round(0.10 + best_score * 0.3, 4)  # Low confidence for UNKNOWN\n        return \"UNKNOWN\", \"\", confidence, f\"weak_evidence;best_score={best_score:.2f}\"\n\n    # Compute confidence from match quality\n    # Ensure separation between UNKNOWN and non-UNKNOWN\n    if best_score >= 0.6:\n        confidence = round(0.75 + (best_score - 0.6) * 0.625, 4)  # 0.75-1.0 range\n    elif best_score >= 0.4:\n        confidence = round(0.55 + (best_score - 0.4) * 1.0, 4)    # 0.55-0.75 range\n    else:\n        confidence = round(0.30 + (best_score - 0.2) * 1.25, 4)   # 0.30-0.55 range\n\n    confidence = min(0.98, max(0.25, confidence))\n\n    # Build detailed rationale\n    rationale = f\"{best_rationale};fail_code:{fail_code};test_item:{test_item}\"\n\n    return best_code, best_entry.standard_label, confidence, rationale\n\ndef process_record(\n    record: dict,\n    codebook: Dict[str, CodebookEntry]\n) -> NormalizedRecord:\n    \"\"\"Process a single record and return normalized result.\"\"\"\n    record_id = record['record_id']\n    product_id = record['product_id']\n    station = record['station']\n    engineer_id = record['engineer_id']\n    raw_reason_text = record['raw_reason_text']\n    fail_code = record.get('fail_code', '')\n    test_item = record.get('test_item', '')\n\n    #        # e.g., \"治具/探针接触不良，重插后偶尔OK 返修 & 治具/探针接触不良...\"\n        # These should be kept together as one segment if they represent the same issue\n\n        # Check if segment contains markers like \"\"\"Compute text similarity score between 0 and 1.\"\"\"\n    t1 = normalize_text(text1)\n    t2 = normalize_text(text2)\n\n    if not t1 or not t2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    # Word overlap\n    words1 = set(re.findall(r'\\w+', t1))\n    words2 = set(re.findall(r'\\w+', t2))\n\n    if not words1 or not words2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = words1 & words2\n    union = words1 | words2\n\n    jaccard = len(intersection) / len(union) if union else 0.0\n\n    # Also check substring containment\n    containment = 0.0\n    if t1 in t2 or t2 in t1:\n        containment = 0.5\n\n    return min(1.0, jaccard + containment)\n\ndef compute_keyword_match(text: str, keywords: List[str]) -> Tuple[float, List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute keyword match score and return matched keywords.\n    Returns (score, list of matched keywords).\n    \"\"\"\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    matched = []\n\n    for kw in keywords:\n        kw_lower = kw.lower()\n        if kw_lower in text_lower:\n            matched.append(kw)\n\n    if not keywords:\n        return 0.0, []\n\n    score = len(matched) / len(keywords)\n    return score, matched\n\ndef detect_defect_type(text: str) -> List[str]:\n    \"\"\"Detect defect types from text based on keywords.\"\"\"\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    detected = []\n\n    for defect_type, keywords in DEFECT_KEYWORDS.items():\n        for kw in keywords:\n            if kw.lower() in text_lower:\n                if defect_type not in detected:\n                    detected.append(defect_type)\n                break\n\n    return match score for a codebook entry against a segment.\n    Returns (score, rationale_hint).\n    \"\"\"\n    score = 0.0\n    rationale_parts = []\n\n    # 1. Station compatibility check (hard filter but also scoring)\n    station_compatible = not entry.station_scope or station in entry.station_scope\n    if not station_compatible:\n        return 0.0, \"station_incompatible\"\n\n    # 2. Text evidence from keywords\n    kw_score, matched_kws = compute_keyword_match(segment_text, entry.keywords)\n    score += kw_score * 0.35\n    if matched_kws:\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"keywords:[{','.join(matched_kws[:2])}]\")\n\n    # 3. Text evidence from standard_label\n    label_sim = compute_text_similarity(segment_text, entry.standard_label)\n    score += label_sim * 0.25\n\n    # 4. Defect type alignment\n    detected_types = detect_defect_type(segment_text)\n    if entry.category_lv2 in detected_types:\n        score += 0.20\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"category:{entry.category_lv2}\")\n\n    # 5. Component match (if label has component reference)\n    segment_components = extract_components(segment_text)\n    label_components = extract_components(entry.standard_label)\n    if segment_components and label_components:\n        common = set(segment_components) & set(label_components)\n        if common:\n            score += 0.15\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"component:{list(common)[0]}\")\n\n    # 6. Station bonus\n    if station_compatible and entry.station_scope:\n        score += 0.05\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"station:{station}\")\n\n    # Normalize score to [0, 1]\n    score = min(1.0, score)\n\n    rationale = '; '.join(rationale_parts) if rationale_parts else 'weak_match'\n    return score, rationale\n\ndef deterministic_tiebreak(record_id: str, segment_idx: int, codes: List[str]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Deterministic tie-break among near-best candidates.\n    Uses hash of context to select consistently.\n    \"\"\"\n    context = f\"{record_id}_{segment_idx}\"\n    hash_val = int(hashlib.md5(context.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n    return codes[hash_val % len(codes)]\n\ndef find_best_match(\n    segment_text: str,\n    codebook: Dict[str, CodebookEntry],\n    station: str,\n    fail_code: str,\n    test_item: str,\n    record_id: str,\n    segment_idx: int\n) -> Tuple[str, str, float, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Find best matching codebook entry for a segment.\n    Returns (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale).\n    \"\"\"\n    candidates = []\n\n    for code, entry in codebook.items():\n        score, rationale = compute_match_score(segment_text, entry, station, fail_code, test_item)\n        if score > 0:\n            candidates.append((code, entry, score, rationale))\n\n    if not candidates:\n        # No matches at all - UNKNOWN\n        return \"UNKNOWN\", \"\", 0.15, \"no_matching_candidates\"\n\n    # Sort by score descending\n    candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[2], reverse=True)\n\n    best_code, best_entry, best_score, best_rationale = candidates[0]\n\n    # Check for near-ties (within 0.05 margin)\n    near_best = [c for c in candidates if c[2] >= best_score - 0.05]\n\n    if len(near_best) > 1:\n        # Apply deterministic tie-break\n        near_codes = [c[0] for c in near_best]\n        chosen_code = deterministic_tiebreak(record_id, segment_idx, near_codes)\n        for c in near_best:\n            if c[0] == chosen_code:\n                best_code, best_entry, best_score, best_rationale = c\n                break\n\n    # UNKNOWN handling: if best score is too low, mark as UNKNOWN\n    unknown_threshold = 0.20\n\n    if best_score < unknown_threshold:\n        # Weak evidence - output UNKNOWN\n        confidence = round(0.10 + best_score * 0.3, 4)  # Low confidence for UNKNOWN\n        return \"UNKNOWN\", \"\", confidence, f\"weak_evidence;best_score={best_score:.2f}\"\n\n    # Compute confidence from match quality\n    # Ensure separation between UNKNOWN and non-UNKNOWN\n    if best_score >= 0.6:\n        confidence = round(0.75 + (best_score - 0.6) * 0.625, 4)  # 0.75-1.0 range\n    elif best_score >= 0.4:\n        confidence = round(0.55 + (best_score - 0.4) * 1.0, 4)    # 0.55-0.75 range\n    else:\n        confidence = round(0.30 + (best_score - 0.2) * 1.25, 4)   # 0.30-0.55 range\n\n    confidence = min(0.98, max(0.25, confidence))\n\n    # Build detailed rationale\n    rationale = f\"{best_rationale};fail_code:{fail_code};test_item:{test_item}\"\n\n    return best_code, best_entry.standard_label, confidence, rationale\n\ndef process_record(\n    record: dict,\n    codebook: Dict[str, CodebookEntry]\n) -> NormalizedRecord:\n    \"\"\"Process a single record and return normalized result.\"\"\"\n    record_id = record['record_id']\n    product_id = record['product_id']\n    station = record['station']\n    engineer_id = record['engineer_id']\n    raw_reason_text = record['raw_reason_text']\n    fail_code = record.get('fail_code', '')\n    test_item = record.get('test_item', '')\n\n    # Output statistics\n    total_segments = len(non_unknown_confidences) + len(unknown_confidences)\n    print(f\"\\nStatistics:\")\n    print(f\"  Total records: {len(results)}\")\n    print(f\"  Total segments: {total_segments}\")\n    print(f\"  UNKNOWN segments: {unknown_count} ({100*unknown_count/total_segments:.1f}%)\")\n\n    if non_unknown_confidences:\n        non_unk_sorted = sorted(non_unknown_confidences)\n        print(f\"  Non-UNKNOWN confidence: mean={sum(non_unknown_confidences)/len(non_unknown_confidences):.4f}, \"\n              f\"Q25={non_unk_sorted[len(non_unk_sorted)//4]:.4f}, \"\n              f\"Q50={non_unk_sorted[len(non_unk_sorted)//2]:.4f}, \"\n              f\"Q75={non_unk_sorted[3*len(non_unk_sorted)//4]:.4f}\")\n\n    if unknown_confidences:\n        unk_sorted = sorted(unknown_confidences)\n        print(f\"  UNKNOWN confidence: mean={sum(unknown_confidences)/len(unknown_confidences):.4f}, \"\n              f\"Q25={unk_sorted[len(unk_sorted)//4]:.4f}, \"\n              f\"Q50={unk_sorted[len(unk_sorted)//2]:.4f}, \"\n              f\"Q75={unk_sorted[3*len(unk_sorted)//4]:.4f}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nWriting output...\")\n    output = {\"records\": results}\n\n    with open('/app/output/solution.json', 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Done! '接触不良', 'reseat', '重插', '治具', '治俱'],\n    'FLASH_FAIL': ['flash', '烧录', 'program', 'write fail', '写入失败', '重烧'],\n    'CHECKSUM_FAIL': ['checksum', 'CRC', 'verify fail', '校验失败', '校验'],\n    'BOOT_FAIL': ['boot', 'OS fail', '启动', 'cannot boot', '开机', 'stuck', 'logo', '卡'],\n    'VERSION_MISMATCH': ['version', '版本', 'wrong FW', '下错程序'],\n    'LEAKAGE': ['hipot', 'leakage', '漏电', '绝缘'],\n    'CURRENT_HIGH': ['current high', 'ICC', '功耗', '静态电流', '电流偏高'],\n    'MISSING': ['missing', '少件', '漏装'],\n    'REVERSED': ['reversed', 'polarity', '反向', '极性'],\n    'PRESSURE_MARK': ['pressure mark', '压伤', 'pad damage'],\n    'ESD': ['ESD', '静电'],\n    'PROBE_WORN': ['probe worn', '探针磨损', 'replace pin', '换针', '磨损'],\n    'CAL_DATA_FAIL': ['cal data', 'RF cal', '校准数据', 'nv write', 'recal'],\n    'CONNECTOR_WEAR': ['connector wear', 'SMA', '插拔', 'insertion loss'],\n    'CONTAMINATION': ['contamination', 'residue', '助焊剂', '清洗', '污染'],\n    'HIGH_RES': ['high_res', 'HIGH_RES'],\n}\n\n# Component pattern to extract component references\nCOMPONENT_PATTERN = re.compile(r'\\b([RCLQUDJTYS]\\d+|ANT\\d*|TP\\d+|SH\\d+)\\b', re.IGNORECASE)\n\ndef extract_components(text: str) -> List[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract component references from text.\"\"\"\n    matches = COMPONENT_PATTERN.findall(text)\n    return [m.upper() for m in matches]\n\ndef compute_text_similarity(text1: str, text2: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Compute text similarity score between 0 and 1.\"\"\"\n    t1 = normalize_text(text1)\n    t2 = normalize_text(text2)\n\n    if not t1 or not t2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    # Word overlap\n    words1 = set(re.findall(r'\\w+', t1))\n    words2 = set(re.findall(r'\\w+', t2))\n\n    if not words1 or not words2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = words1 & words2\n    union = words1 | words2\n\n    jaccard = len(intersection) / len(union) if union else 0.0\n\n    # Also check substring containment\n    containment = 0.0\n    if t1 in t2 or t2 in t1:\n        containment = 0.5\n\n    return min(1.0, jaccard + containment)\n\ndef compute_keyword_match(text: str, keywords: List[str]) -> Tuple[float, List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute keyword match score and return matched keywords.\n    Returns (score, list of matched keywords).\n    \"\"\"\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    matched = []\n\n    for kw in keywords:\n        kw_lower = kw.lower()\n        if kw_lower in text_lower:\n            matched.append(kw)\n\n    if not keywords:\n        return 0.0, []\n\n    score = len(matched) / len(keywords)\n    return score, matched\n\ndef detect_defect_type(text: str) -> List[str]:\n    \"\"\"Detect defect types from text based on keywords.\"\"\"\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    detected = []\n\n    for defect_type, keywords in DEFECT_KEYWORDS.items():\n        for kw in keywords:\n            if kw.lower() in text_lower:\n                if defect_type not in detected:\n                    detected.append(defect_type)\n                break\n\n    return detected\n\ndef compute_match_score(\n    segment_text: str,\n    entry: CodebookEntry,\n    station: str,\n    fail_code: str,\n    test_item: str\n) -> Tuple[float, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute match score for a codebook entry against a segment.\n    Returns (score, rationale_hint).\n    \"\"\"\n    score = 0.0\n    rationale_parts = []\n\n    # 1. Station compatibility check (hard filter but also scoring)\n    station_compatible = not entry.station_scope or station in entry.station_scope\n    if not station_compatible:\n        return 0.0, \"station_incompatible\"\n\n    # 2. Text evidence from keywords\n    kw_score, matched_kws = compute_keyword_match(segment_text, entry.keywords)\n    score += kw_score * 0.35\n    if matched_kws:\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"keywords:[{','.join(matched_kws[:2])}]\")\n\n    # 3. Text evidence from standard_label\n    label_sim = compute_text_similarity(segment_text, entry.standard_label)\n    score += label_sim * 0.25\n\n    # 4. Defect type alignment\n    detected_types = detect_defect_type(segment_text)\n    if entry.category_lv2 in detected_types:\n        score += 0.20\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"category:{entry.category_lv2}\")\n\n    # 5. Component match (if label has component reference)\n    segment_components = extract_components(segment_text)\n    label_components = extract_components(entry.standard_label)\n    if segment_components and label_components:\n        common = set(segment_components) & set(label_components)\n        if common:\n            score += 0.15\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"component:{list(common)[0]}\")\n\n    # 6. Station bonus\n    if station_compatible and entry.station_scope:\n        score += 0.05\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"station:{station}\")\n\n    # Normalize score to [0, 1]\n    score = min(1.0, score)\n\n    rationale = '; '.join(rationale_parts) if rationale_parts else 'weak_match'\n    return score, rationale\n\ndef deterministic_tiebreak(record_id: str, segment_idx: int, codes: List[str]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Deterministic tie-break among near-best candidates.\n    Uses hash of context to select consistently.\n    \"\"\"\n    context = f\"{record_id}_{segment_idx}\"\n    hash_val = int(hashlib.md5(context.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n    return codes[hash_val % len(codes)]\n\ndef find_best_match(\n    segment_text: str,\n    codebook: Dict[str, CodebookEntry],\n    station: str,\n    fail_code: str,\n    test_item: str,\n    record_id: str,\n    segment_idx: int\n) -> Tuple[str, str, float, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Find best matching codebook entry for a segment.\n    Returns (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale).\n    \"\"\"\n    candidates = []\n\n    for code, entry in codebook.items():\n        score, rationale = compute_match_score(segment_text, entry, station, fail_code, test_item)\n        if score > 0:\n            candidates.append((code, entry, score, rationale))\n\n    if not candidates:\n        # No matches at all - UNKNOWN\n        return \"UNKNOWN\", \"\", 0.15, \"no_matching_candidates\"\n\n    # Sort by score descending\n    candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[2], reverse=True)\n\n    best_code, best_entry, best_score, best_rationale = candidates[0]\n\n    # Check for near-ties (within 0.05 margin)\n    near_best = [c for c in candidates if c[2] >= best_score - 0.05]\n\n    if len(near_best) > 1:\n        # Apply deterministic tie-break\n        near_codes = [c[0] for c in near_best]\n        chosen_code = deterministic_tiebreak(record_id, segment_idx, near_codes)\n        for c in near_best:\n            if c[0] == chosen_code:\n                best_code, best_entry, best_score, best_rationale = c\n                break\n\n    # UNKNOWN handling: if best score is too low, mark as UNKNOWN\n    unknown_threshold = 0.20\n\n    if best_score < unknown_threshold:\n        # Weak evidence - output UNKNOWN\n        confidence = round(0.10 + best_score * 0.3, 4)  # Low confidence for UNKNOWN\n        return \"UNKNOWN\", \"\", confidence, f\"weak_evidence;best_score={best_score:.2f}\"\n\n    # Compute confidence from match quality\n    # Ensure separation between UNKNOWN and non-UNKNOWN\n    if best_score >= 0.6:\n        confidence = round(0.75 + (best_score - 0.6) * 0.625, 4)  # 0.75-1.0 range\n    elif best_score >= 0.4:\n        confidence = round(0.55 + (best_score - 0.4) * 1.0, 4)    # 0.55-0.75 range\n    else:\n        confidence = round(0.30 + (best_score - 0.2) * 1.25, 4)   # 0.30-0.55 range\n\n    confidence = min(0.98, max(0.25, confidence))\n\n    # Build detailed rationale\n    rationale = f\"{best_rationale};fail_code:{fail_code};test_item:{test_item}\"\n\n    return best_code, best_entry.standard_label, confidence, rationale\n\ndef process_record(\n    record: dict,\n    codebook: Dict[str, CodebookEntry]\n) -> NormalizedRecord:\n    \"\"\"Process a single record and return normalized result.\"\"\"\n    record_id = record['record_id']\n    product_id = record['product_id']\n    station = record['station']\n    engineer_id = record['engineer_id']\n    raw_reason_text = record['raw_reason_text']\n    fail_code = record.get('fail_code', '')\n    test_item = record.get('test_item', '')\n\n    # if phrase.lower() in text.lower():\n            # Find the actual case in text\n            idx = text.lower().find(phrase.lower())\n            actual = text[idx:idx+len(phrase)]\n            text = text[:idx] + placeholder + text[idx+len(phrase):]\n            placeholders[placeholder] = actual\n    return text, placeholders\n\ndef restore_phrases(segments: List[str], placeholders: Dict[str, seg = seg.replace(placeholder, original)\n        result.append(seg)\n    return result\n\ndef segment_reason_text(raw_text: str) -> List[str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Split raw_reason_text into segments Protect phrases that shouldn't be split\n    protected_text, placeholders = protect_phrases(raw_text)\n\n    # Combined delimiter pattern\n    delimiter_pattern = '|'.join(SEGMENT_DELIMITERS)\n\n    # Split by delimiters\n    segments = re.split(delimiter_pattern, protected_text)\n\n    # Restore protected phrases\n    segments = restore_phrases(segments, placeholders)\n\n    # Clean up segments\n    result = []\n    for seg in segments:\n        seg = seg.strip()\n        if not seg:\n            continue\n        # Remove trailing status markers that don't add info\n        seg = re.sub(r'\\s+(NG|OK了|已处理|有问题|疑似|换掉|重测过|已处理|接触不好|不稳定|返修|挂|浮高|可能|suspect|intermittent|rework|retest)$', '', seg, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n        if seg.strip():\n            result.append(seg.strip())\n\n    # If no segments found, return original text\n    if not result:\n        result = [raw_text.strip()]\n\n    return result\n\n# Keyword mappings for different defect types\nDEFECT_KEYWORDS = {\n extract_components(text: str) -> List[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract component references from text.\"\"\"\n    matches = COMPONENT_PATTERN.findall(text)\n    return [m.upper() for m in matches]\n\ndef compute_text_similarity(text1: str, text2: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Compute text similarity score between 0 and 1.\"\"\"\n    t1 = normalize_text(text1)\n    t2 = normalize_text(text2)\n\n    if not t1 or not t2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    # Word overlap\n    words1 = set(re.findall(r'\\w+', t1))\n    words2 = set(re.findall(r'\\w+', t2))\n\n    if not words1 or not words2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = words1 & words2\n    union = words1 | words2\n\n    jaccard = len(intersection) / len(union) if union else 0.0\n\n    # Also check substring containment\n    containment = 0.0\n    if t1 in t2 or t2 in t1:\n        containment = 0.5\n\n    return min(1.0, jaccard + containment)\n\ndef compute_keyword_match(text: str, keywords: List[str]) -> Tuple[float, List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute keyword match score and return matched keywords.\n    Returns (score, list of matched keywords).\n    \"\"\"\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    matched = []\n\n    for kw in keywords:\n        kw_lower = kw.lower()\n        if kw_lower in text_lower:\n            matched.append(kw)\n\n    if not keywords:\n        return 0.0, []\n\n    score = len(matched) / len(keywords)\n    return score, matched\n\ndef detect_defect_type(text: str) -> List[str]:\n    \"\"\"Detect defect types from text based on keywords.\"\"\"\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    detected = []\n\n    for defect_type, keywords in DEFECT_KEYWORDS.items():\n        for kw in keywords:\n            if kw.lower() in text_lower:\n                if defect_type not in detected:\n                    detected.append(defect_type)\n                break\n\n    return detected\n\ndef compute_match_score(\n    segment_text: str,\n    entry: CodebookEntry,\n    station: str,\n    fail_code: str,\n    test_item: str\n) -> Tuple[float, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute match score for a codebook entry against a segment.\n    Returns (score, rationale_hint).\n    \"\"\"\n    score = 0.0\n    rationale_parts = []\n\n    # 1. Station compatibility check (hard filter but also scoring)\n    station_compatible = not entry.station_scope or station in entry.station_scope\n    if not station_compatible:\n        return 0.0, \"station_incompatible\"\n\n    # 2. Text evidence from keywords\n    kw_score, matched_kws = compute_keyword_match(segment_text, entry.keywords)\n    score += kw_score * 0.35\n    if matched_kws:\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"keywords:[{','.join(matched_kws[:2])}]\")\n\n    # 3. Text evidence from standard_label\n    label_sim = compute_text_similarity(segment_text, entry.standard_label)\n    score += label_sim * 0.25\n\n    # 4. Defect type alignment\n    detected_types = detect_defect_type(segment_text)\n    if entry.category_lv2 in detected_types:\n        score += 0.20\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"category:{entry.category_lv2}\")\n\n    # 5. Component match (if label has component reference)\n    segment_components = extract_components(segment_text)\n    label_components = extract_components(entry.standard_label)\n    if segment_components and label_components:\n        common = set(segment_components) & set(label_components)\n        if common:\n            score += 0.15\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"component:{list(common)[0]}\")\n\n    # 6. Station bonus\n    if station_compatible and entry.station_scope:\n        score += 0.05\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"station:{station}\")\n\n    # Normalize score to [0, 1]\n    score = min(1.0, score)\n\n    rationale = '; '.join(rationale_parts) if rationale_parts else 'weak_match'\n    return score, rationale\n\ndef deterministic_tiebreak(record_id: str, segment_idx: int, codes: List[str]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Deterministic tie-break among near-best candidates.\n    Uses hash of context to select consistently.\n    \"\"\"\n    context = f\"{record_id}_{segment_idx}\"\n    hash_val = int(hashlib.md5(context.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n    return codes[hash_val % len(codes)]\n\ndef find_best_match(\n    segment_text: str,\n    codebook: Dict[str, CodebookEntry],\n    station: str,\n    fail_code: str,\n    test_item: str,\n    record_id: str,\n    segment_idx: int\n) -> Tuple[str, str, float, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Find best matching codebook entry for a segment.\n    Returns (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale).\n    \"\"\"\n    candidates = []\n\n    for code, entry in codebook.items():\n        score, rationale = compute_match_score(segment_text, entry, station, fail_code, test_item)\n        if score > 0:\n            candidates.append((code, entry, score, rationale))\n\n    if not candidates:\n        # No matches at all - UNKNOWN\n        return \"UNKNOWN\", \"\", 0.15, \"no_matching_candidates\"\n\n    # Sort by score descending\n    candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[2], reverse=True)\n\n    best_code, best_entry, best_score, best_rationale = candidates[0]\n\n    # Check for near-ties (within 0.05 margin)\n    near_best = [c for c in candidates if c[2] >= best_score - 0.05]\n\n    if len(near_best) > 1:\n        # Apply deterministic tie-break\n        near_codes = [c[0] for c in near_best]\n        chosen_code = deterministic_tiebreak(record_id, segment_idx, near_codes)\n        for c in near_best:\n            if c[0] == chosen_code:\n                best_code, best_entry, best_score, best_rationale = c\n                break\n\n    # UNKNOWN handling: if best score is too low, mark as UNKNOWN\n    unknown_threshold = 0.20\n\n    if best_score < unknown_threshold:\n        # Weak evidence - output UNKNOWN\n        confidence = round(0.10 + best_score * 0.3, 4)  # Low confidence for UNKNOWN\n        return \"UNKNOWN\", \"\", confidence, f\"weak_evidence;best_score={best_score:.2f}\"\n\n    # Compute confidence from match quality\n    # Ensure separation between UNKNOWN and non-UNKNOWN\n    if best_score >= 0.6:\n        confidence = round(0.75 + (best_score - 0.6) * 0.625, 4)  # 0.75-1.0 range\n    elif best_score >= 0.4:\n        confidence = round(0.55 + (best_score - 0.4) * 1.0, 4)    # 0.55-0.75 range\n    else:\n        confidence = round(0.30 + (best_score - 0.2) * 1.25, 4)   # 0.30-0.55 range\n\n    confidence = min(0.98, max(0.25, confidence))\n\n    # Build detailed rationale\n    rationale = f\"{best_rationale};fail_code:{fail_code};test_item:{test_item}\"\n\n    return best_code, best_entry.standard_label, confidence, rationale\n\ndef process_record(\n    record: dict,\n    codebook: Dict[str, CodebookEntry]\n) -> NormalizedRecord:\n    \"\"\"Process a single record and return normalized result.\"\"\"\n    record_id = record['record_id']\n    product_id = record['product_id']\n    station = record['station']\n    engineer_id = record['engineer_id']\n    raw_reason_text = record['raw_reason_text']\n    fail_code = record.get('fail_code', '')\n    test_item = record.get('test_item', '')\n\n    # Segment the raw text\n    segments = segment_reason_text(raw_reason_text)\n\n    # Deduplicate consecutive identical segments\n    deduped_segments = []\n    for seg in segments:\n        seg = seg.strip()\n        if seg and (not deduped_segments or normalize_text(seg) != normalize_text(deduped_segments[-1])):\n            deduped_segments.append(seg)\n\n    if not deduped_segments:\n        deduped_segments = [raw_reason_text if raw_reason_text else \"\"]\n\n    normalized_segments = []\n    for idx, span_text in enumerate(deduped_segments, start=1):\n        segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{idx}\"\n\n        pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale = find_best_match(\n            span_text,\n            codebook,\n            station,\n            fail_code,\n            test_item,\n            record_id,\n            idx\n        )\n\n        normalized_segments.append(NormalizedSegment(\n            segment_id=segment_id,\n            span_text=span_text,\n            pred_code=pred_code,\n            pred_label=pred_label,\n            confidence=confidence,\n            rationale=rationale\n        ))\n\n    return NormalizedRecord(\n        record_id=record_id,\n        product_id=product_id,\n        station=station,\n        engineer_id=engineer_id,\n        raw_reason_text=raw_reason_text,\n        normalized=normalized_segments\n    )\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading codebooks...\")\n    codebooks_raw = load_all_codebooks()\n\n    # Deduplicate codebooks\n    codebooks = {}\n    for product_id, cb in codebooks_raw.items():\n        codebooks[product_id] = deduplicate_codebook(cb)\n        print(f\"  {product_id}: {len(codebooks[product_id])} unique entries\")\n\n    print(\"Loading test center logs...\")\n    logs = load_logs('/app/data/test_center_logs.csv')\n    print(f\"  Loaded {len(logs)} records\")\n\n    print(\"Processing records...\")\n    results = []\n    unknown_count = 0\n    non_unknown_confidences = []\n    unknown_confidences = []\n\n    for i, record in enumerate(logs):\n        product_id = record['product_id']\n\n        if product_id not in codebooks:\n            print(f\"  Warning: Unknown product_id {product_id} in record {record['record_id']}\")\n            continue\n\n        codebook = codebooks[product_id]\n        normalized = process_record(record, codebook)\n\n        # Output statistics\n    total_segments = len(non_unknown_confidences) + len(unknown_confidences)\n    print(f\"\\nStatistics:\")\n    print(f\"  Total records: {len(results)}\")\n    print(f\"  Total segments: {total_segments}\")\n    print(f\"  UNKNOWN segments: {unknown_count} ({100*unknown_count/total_segments:.1f}%)\")\n\n    if non_unknown_confidences:\n        non_unk_sorted = sorted(non_unknown_confidences)\n        print(f\"  Non-UNKNOWN confidence: mean={sum(non_unknown_confidences)/len(non_unknown_confidences):.4f}, \"\n              f\"Q25={non_unk_sorted[len(non_unk_sorted)//4]:.4f}, \"\n              f\"Q50={non_unk_sorted[len(non_unk_sorted)//2]:.4f}, \"\n              f\"Q75={non_unk_sorted[3*len(non_unk_sorted)//4]:.4f}\")\n\n    if unknown_confidences:\n        unk_sorted = sorted(unknown_confidences)\n        print(f\"  UNKNOWN confidence: mean={sum(unknown_confidences)/len(unknown_confidences):.4f}, \"\n              f\"Q25={unk_sorted[len(unk_sorted)//4]:.4f}, \"\n              f\"Q50={unk_sorted[len(unk_sorted)//2]:.4f}, \"\n              f\"Q75={unk_sorted[3*len(unk_sorted)//4]:.4f}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nWriting output...\")\n    output = {\"records\": results}\n\n    with open('/app/output/solution.json', 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Done! rationale_parts = []\n\n    # 1. Text evidence from keywords\n    kw_score, matched_kws = compute_keyword_match(segment_text, entry.keywords)\n    score += kw_score * 0.35\n    if matched_kws:\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"keywords:[{','.join(matched_kws[:2])}]\")\n\n    # 3. Text evidence from standard_label\n    label_sim = compute_text_similarity(segment_text, entry.standard_label)\n    score += label_sim * 0.25\n\n    # 4. Defect type alignment\n    detected_types = detect_defect_type(segment_text)\n    if entry.category_lv2 in detected_types:\n        score += 0.20\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"category:{entry.category_lv2}\")\n\n    # 5. Station bonus\n    if station_compatible and entry.station_scope:\n        score += 0.05\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"station:{station}\")\n\n    # Normalize score to [0, 1]\n    score = min(1.0, score)\n\n    rationale = '; '.join(rationale_parts) if rationale_parts else 'weak_match'\n    return score, rationale\n\ndef deterministic_tiebreak(record_id: str, segment_idx: int, codes: List[str]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Deterministic tie-break among near-best candidates.\n    Uses hash of context to select consistently.\n    \"\"\"\n    context = f\"{record_id}_{segment_idx}\"\n    hash_val = int(hashlib.md5(context.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n    return codes[hash_val % len(codes)]\n\ndef find_best_match(\n    segment_text: str,\n    codebook: Dict[str, CodebookEntry],\n    station: str,\n    fail_code: str,\n    test_item: str,\n    record_id: str,\n    segment_idx: int\n) -> Tuple[str, str, float, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Find best matching codebook entry for a segment.\n    Returns (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale).\n    \"\"\"\n    candidates = []\n\n    for code, entry in codebook.items():\n        score, rationale = compute_match_score(segment_text, entry, station, fail_code, test_item)\n        if score > 0:\n            candidates.append((code, entry, score, rationale))\n\n    if not candidates:\n        # 0.75-1.0 range\n    elif best_score >= 0.4:\n        confidence = round(0.55 + (best_score - 0.4) * 1.0, 4)    # 0.55-0.75 range\n    else:\n        confidence = round(0.30 + (best_score - 0.2) * 1.25, 4)   # (score, rationale_hint).\n    \"\"\"\n    score = 0.0\n    rationale_parts = []\n\n    # 1. Station compatibility check (hard filter but also scoring)\n    station_compatible = not entry.station_scope or station in entry.station_scope\n    if not station_compatible:\n        return 0.0, \"station_incompatible\"\n\n    # Expand segment text with synonyms for better matching\n    expanded_segment = expand_text_with_synonyms(segment_text)\n\n    # 2. Text evidence from keywords - use expanded text\n    kw_score, matched_kws = compute_keyword_match(expanded_segment, entry.keywords)\n    score += kw_score * 0.40\n    if matched_kws:\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"keywords:[{','.join(matched_kws[:2])}]\")\n\n    # 3. Text evidence from standard_label - use expanded text\n    label_sim = compute_text_similarity(expanded_segment, entry.standard_label)\n    score += label_sim * 0.25\n\n    # 4. Defect type alignment - strong signal\n    detected_types = detect_defect_type(expanded_segment)\n    if entry.category_lv2 in detected_types:\n        score += 0.25\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"category:{entry.category_lv2}\")\n\n    # 5. Component match (if label has component reference)\n    segment_components = extract_components(segment_text)\n    label_components = extract_components(entry.standard_label)\n    if segment_components and label_components:\n        common = set(segment_components) & set(label_components)\n        if common:\n            score += 0.10\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"component:{list(common)[0]}\")\n\n    # 6. Station compatibility bonus (already checked above)\n    if station_compatible and entry.station_scope:\n        score += 0.05\n\n    # Normalize score to [0, 1]\n    score = min(1.0, score)\n\n    # Add station to rationale\n    rationale_parts.append(f\"station:{station}\")\n\n    rationale = '; '.join(rationale_parts) if rationale_parts else 'weak_match'\n    return score, rationale\n\ndef deterministic_tiebreak(record_id: str, segment_idx: int, codes: List[str]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Deterministic tie-break among near-best candidates.\n    Uses hash of context to select consistently.\n    \"\"\"\n    context = f\"{record_id}_{segment_idx}\"\n    hash_val = int(hashlib.md5(context.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n    return codes[hash_val % len(codes)]\n\ndef find_best_match(\n    segment_text: str,\n    codebook: Dict[str, CodebookEntry],\n    station: str,\n    fail_code: str,\n    test_item: str,\n    record_id: str,\n    segment_idx: int\n) -> Tuple[str, str, float, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Find best matching codebook entry for a segment.\n    Returns (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale).\n    \"\"\"\n    candidates = []\n\n    for code, entry in codebook.items():\n        score, rationale = compute_match_score(segment_text, entry, station, fail_code, test_item)\n        if score > 0:\n            candidates.append((code, entry, score, rationale))\n\n    if not candidates:\n        # No matches at all - UNKNOWN\n        return \"UNKNOWN\", \"\", 0.15, \"no_matching_candidates\"\n\n    # Sort by score descending\n    candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[2], reverse=True)\n\n    best_code, best_entry, best_score, best_rationale = candidates[0]\n\n    # Check for near-ties (within 0.05 margin)\n    near_best = [c for c in candidates if c[2] >= best_score - 0.05]\n\n    if len(near_best) > 1:\n        # Apply deterministic tie-break\n        near_codes = [c[0] for c in near_best]\n        chosen_code = deterministic_tiebreak(record_id, segment_idx, near_codes)\n        for c in near_best:\n            if c[0] == chosen_code:\n                best_code, best_entry, best_score, best_rationale = c\n                break\n\n    # UNKNOWN handling: if best score is too low, mark as UNKNOWN\n    unknown_threshold = 0.20\n\n    if best_score < unknown_threshold:\n        # Weak evidence - output UNKNOWN\n        confidence = round(0.10 + best_score * 0.3, 4)  # Low confidence for UNKNOWN\n        return \"UNKNOWN\", \"\", confidence, f\"weak_evidence;best_score={best_score:.2f}\"\n\n    # Compute confidence from match quality\n    # Ensure separation between UNKNOWN and non-UNKNOWN\n    if best_score >= 0.6:\n        confidence = round(0.75 + (best_score - 0.6) * 0.625, 4)  # 0.75-1.0 range\n    elif best_score >= 0.4:\n        confidence = round(0.55 + (best_score - 0.4) * 1.0, 4)    # 0.55-0.75 range\n    else:\n        confidence = round(0.30 + (best_score - 0.2) * 1.25, 4)   # 0.30-0.55 range\n\n    confidence = min(0.98, max(0.25, confidence))\n\n    # Build detailed rationale\n    rationale = f\"{best_rationale};fail_code:{fail_code};test_item:{test_item}\"\n\n    return best_code, best_entry.standard_label, confidence, rationale\n\ndef process_record(\n    record: dict,\n    codebook: Dict[str, CodebookEntry]\n) -> NormalizedRecord:\n    \"\"\"Process a single record and return normalized result.\"\"\"\n    record_id = record['record_id']\n    product_id = record['product_id']\n    station = record['station']\n    engineer_id = record['engineer_id']\n    raw_reason_text = record['raw_reason_text']\n    fail_code = record.get('fail_code', '')\n    test_item = record.get('test_item', '')\n\n    # Segment the raw text\n    segments = segment_reason_text(raw_reason_text)\n\n    # Deduplicate consecutive identical segments\n    deduped_segments = []\n    for seg in segments:\n        seg = seg.strip()\n        if seg and (not deduped_segments or normalize_text(seg) != normalize_text(deduped_segments[-1])):\n            deduped_segments.append(seg)\n\n    if not deduped_segments:\n        deduped_segments = [raw_reason_text if raw_reason_text else \"\"]\n\n    normalized_segments = []\n    for idx, span_text in enumerate(deduped_segments, start=1):\n        segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{idx}\"\n\n        pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale = find_best_match(\n            span_text,\n            codebook,\n            station,\n            fail_code,\n            test_item,\n            record_id,\n            idx\n        )\n\n        normalized_segments.append(NormalizedSegment(\n            segment_id=segment_id,\n            span_text=span_text,\n            pred_code=pred_code,\n            pred_label=pred_label,\n            confidence=confidence,\n            rationale=rationale\n        ))\n\n    return NormalizedRecord(\n        record_id=record_id,\n        product_id=product_id,\n        station=station,\n        engineer_id=engineer_id,\n        raw_reason_text=raw_reason_text,\n        normalized=normalized_segments\n    )\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading codebooks...\")\n    codebooks_raw = load_all_codebooks()\n\n    # Deduplicate codebooks\n    codebooks = {}\n    for product_id, cb in codebooks_raw.items():\n        codebooks[product_id] = deduplicate_codebook(cb)\n        print(f\"  {product_id}: {len(codebooks[product_id])} unique entries\")\n\n    print(\"Loading test center logs...\")\n    logs = load_logs('/app/data/test_center_logs.csv')\n    print(f\"  Loaded {len(logs)} records\")\n\n    print(\"Processing records...\")\n    results = []\n    unknown_count = 0\n    non_unknown_confidences = []\n    unknown_confidences = []\n\n    for i, record in enumerate(logs):\n        product_id = record['product_id']\n\n        if product_id not in codebooks:\n            print(f\"  Warning: Unknown product_id {product_id} in record {record['record_id']}\")\n            continue\n\n        codebook = codebooks[product_id]\n        normalized = process_record(record, codebook)\n\n        # Convert to dict for JSON output\n        record_dict = {\n            \"record_id\": normalized.record_id,\n            \"product_id\": normalized.product_id,\n            \"station\": normalized.station,\n            \"engineer_id\": normalized.engineer_id,\n            \"raw_reason_text\": normalized.raw_reason_text,\n            \"normalized\": []\n        }\n\n        for seg in normalized.normalized:\n            seg_dict = {\n                \"segment_id\": seg.segment_id,\n                \"span_text\": seg.span_text,\n                \"pred_code\": seg.pred_code,\n                \"pred_label\": seg.pred_label,\n                \"confidence\": seg.confidence,\n                \"rationale\": seg.rationale\n            }\n            record_dict[\"normalized\"].append(seg_dict)\n\n            if seg.pred_code == \"UNKNOWN\":\n                unknown_count += 1\n                unknown_confidences.append(seg.confidence)\n            else:\n                non_unknown_confidences.append(seg.confidence)\n\n        results.append(record_dict)\n\n        if (i + 1) % 2000 == 0:\n            print(f\"  Processed {i + 1}/{len(logs)} records...\")\n\n    # Output statistics\n    total_segments = len(non_unknown_confidences) + len(unknown_confidences)\n    print(f\"\\nStatistics:\")\n    print(f\"  Total records: {len(results)}\")\n    print(f\"  Total segments: {total_segments}\")\n    print(f\"  UNKNOWN segments: {unknown_count} ({100*unknown_count/total_segments:.1f}%)\")\n\n    if non_unknown_confidences:\n        non_unk_sorted = sorted(non_unknown_confidences)\n        print(f\"  Non-UNKNOWN confidence: mean={sum(non_unknown_confidences)/len(non_unknown_confidences):.4f}, \"\n              f\"Q25={non_unk_sorted[len(non_unk_sorted)//4]:.4f}, \"\n              f\"Q50={non_unk_sorted[len(non_unk_sorted)//2]:.4f}, \"\n              f\"Q75={non_unk_sorted[3*len(non_unk_sorted)//4]:.4f}\")\n\n    if unknown_confidences:\n        unk_sorted = sorted(unknown_confidences)\n        print(f\"  UNKNOWN confidence: mean={sum(unknown_confidences)/len(unknown_confidences):.4f}, \"\n              f\"Q25={unk_sorted[len(unk_sorted)//4]:.4f}, \"\n              f\"Q50={unk_sorted[len(unk_sorted)//2]:.4f}, \"\n              f\"Q75={unk_sorted[3*len(unk_sorted)//4]:.4f}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nWriting output...\")\n    output = {\"records\": results}\n\n    with open('/app/output/solution.json', 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Done!
